### D135 — Nuclear Engineering and Radiation Science

Candidate signals: explicit_computational, geospatial_and_remote_sensing, mathematical_modelling, simulation_and_system_modelling, statistics_and_quantitative; 5 source-field hits.
- `domain` `description` (mathematical_modelling): Nuclear engineering and radiation science applies physics to nuclear energy and radiation.
